*Harikrishnan P M,Goutham Vignesh,Ganesh Parab,Saisubramaniam Gopalakrishnan,Vishal Vaddina,Varun V,Rohit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Perception-RFT框架，利用GRPO直接对齐视觉特征与结构化定位输出，发现推理token在训练中被压制，推理策略不如纯感知策略，并识别出新的接地发散现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态文档问答中，显式视觉定位仍具挑战。SFT需要大量标注且易达优化瓶颈，而推理型RL依赖冗长中间推理token，token成本高且收益不明确。

Method: 提出Perception-RFT框架，应用GRPO进行多模态文档问答训练，绕过中间推理token，直接对齐视觉特征与结构化定位输出。构建推理变体进行对比，并进行细粒度优化动力学分析。

Result: 推理模型在训练中抑制推理trace，收敛为直接感知策略，推理token减少超60%；推理型RL表现不如纯感知训练。在4B规模下，SFT饱和与冷启动RL不稳定性在多模态中复现；识别出接地发散现象（语义鲁棒性与几何精度之间的选择性权衡）。早期SFT→RL过渡用65%更少数据达到可比精度。

Conclusion: 感知型RL在多模态文档问答中优于推理型RL；接地发散是联合RL优化下的新问题；早期SFT→RL过渡策略可显著减少训练数据需求。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal document question answering with explicit visual grounding, locating the precise document region that supports each answer remains an open challenge. Current approaches bifurcate into Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), which requires large annotated datasets and reaches optimization plateaus, and reasoning-centric Reinforcement Learning (RL), which depends on verbose intermediate traces that inflate inference token cost without clear benefit. We introduce Perception-RFT, a training framework that applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to multimodal document QA, bypassing intermediate reasoning tokens to directly align visual features with structured grounding outputs. To rigorously evaluate the necessity of reasoning, we construct a reasoning variant under identical reward settings. We find that reasoning-enabled models suppress their reasoning traces during training, converging to direct perception-based policies at the 4B parameter scale, reducing per-query inference token length by more than 60%, while reasoning-enabled RL underperforms perception-only training. Through a fine-grained analysis of Qwen3-VL-4B optimization dynamics, we confirm that SFT saturation and cold-start RL instability established in text-domain post-training extend to multimodal, and identify a previously uncharacterized Grounding Divergence: a selective trade-off between semantic robustness and geometric precision on two out of distribution (OOD) benchmarks (4,828 samples) under joint RL optimization. We further show that an early SFT$\rightarrow$RL transition achieves comparable precision with 65% less training data.

</details>


### [213] [InCarEmo: A Multimodal Dataset for In-Cabin Emotion Recognition and Driver State Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14683)
*Hao Yang,Yanyan Zhao,Kewei Zhao,Hongbo Zhang,Tian Zheng,Yusheng Liu,Xing Fu,Bichen Wang,Yu Zhang,Hao He,Zhen Wu,Xuda Zhi,Yongbo Huang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为InCarEmo的多模态数据集，用于车载情绪识别和驾驶员状态监测。该数据集整合了RGB和红外视频、车载音频及对话文本，覆盖多种光照和驾驶场景，支持情绪识别、疲劳检测和分心监测三个任务，并提供了跨语言评估基准。实验结果证明了多模态融合的优势，也揭示了现实噪声和低光照条件下的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车载情感计算公开数据集主要集中在视觉模态，很少包含对话信息，难以捕捉驾驶员情绪背后的语言和交互线索。

Method: 构建了InCarEmo多模态数据集，整合RGB和红外视频、车载音频和对话文本，通过脚本化车内场景模拟真实驾驶员行为，覆盖不同光照和驾驶情境。提供中文原始数据和辅助英文基准，支持跨语言评估。建立了统一基准，包含单模态和多模态方法的基线结果，并分析了模态缺失和噪声条件下的表现。

Result: 多模态融合方法优于单模态方法，但在现实噪声和低光照条件下仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: InCarEmo为鲁棒、可解释、以人为中心的车内情感理解奠定了全面基础，促进了更安全、更具共情能力的驾驶员-车辆交互。

Abstract: Understanding driver emotion and state is critical for the next generation of intelligent in-cabin systems that ensure safety and enhance human-vehicle interaction. However, existing public datasets for in-cabin affective computing are largely limited to visual modalities and rarely include conversational information, making it difficult to capture the linguistic and interactive cues underlying driver emotion. To address these gaps, we introduce InCarEmo, a multimodal dataset for in-cabin emotion recognition and driver state monitoring. InCarEmo integrates RGB and infrared video, in-cabin audio, and dialogue text collected from scripted in-cabin scenarios designed to simulate realistic driver behaviors, covering diverse lighting conditions and driving contexts. The dataset supports three primary tasks: 1) multimodal emotion recognition, 2) fatigue detection, and 3) distraction monitoring. In addition to the original Chinese data, we construct an auxiliary English benchmark to support preliminary cross-lingual evaluation. We provide a unified benchmark with extensive baseline results across unimodal and multimodal methods, including analyses under modality-missing and noise conditions. Experimental results demonstrate the benefits of multimodal fusion and reveal remaining challenges under real-world noise and low-light conditions. By releasing InCarEmo, we aim to establish a comprehensive foundation for robust, interpretable, and human-centric in-cabin affective understanding, promoting safer and more empathetic driver-vehicle interaction.

</details>


### [214] [AI vs Human Expert Reasoning: Assessing Agreements in Building Typology Predictions based on Street View Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14756)
*Zahratu Shabrina,Muhammad Asa,Jin Rui,Lu Yin,Stephen Law*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探究了视觉语言模型（VLM）从谷歌街景图像中推断建筑类型（结构、当前用途、楼层数）的能力，发现VLM在约70%准确率下可近似人类专家表现，但AI更关注视觉特征，而人类专家更依赖上下文线索和领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索VLM在建筑类型分类中的潜力，评估其是否能够替代或辅助人类专家在城市场景中执行大规模建筑类型推断任务，从而提升城市分析的效率和可扩展性。

Method: 使用GSV图像作为输入，比较多个VLM（GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash）与人类专家（土木工程师和建筑师）的建筑类型分类结果；采用不同缩放策略和提示技术，并利用Chain-of-Thought提示；通过分析AI解释中关键词的出现概率来探究推理模式。

Result: CoT提示策略提供了更稳定的模型性能；VLM平均准确率约70%；AI侧重于视觉指标，而人类专家更依赖上下文线索和领域知识；通过关键词概率分析识别了AI与专家之间一致/不一致的关键主题。

Conclusion: VLM具备在大规模建筑类型分类中近似人类专家能力，可作为城市分析中补充和协作的工具，有助于推动城市分析自动化的发展。

Abstract: This research investigates the potential of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to infer building typologies: Construction, Current Use, and Storeys from Google Street View (GSV) images. Predictions generated by VLMs are compared with inference by human experts (civil engineers and architects) as a source of manually labelled ground-truth data. We evaluate several state-of-the-art VLMs, including GPT-4o, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, and Gemini 2.0 Flash. By applying different scaling strategies and prompting techniques, we found that Chain-of-Thought prompts provide an overall more stable model performance. We also investigate the reasoning behind VLMs' building-typology predictions by examining the probabilities of keywords appearing in AI explanations. This enabled us to analyse patterns in these reasonings and identify key themes driving both agreements and disagreements between VLM and expert labels. We find that AI tends to focus on visual indicators, whereas human experts place greater emphasis on broader contextual cues and domain knowledge, in addition to visual cues. Overall, VLM can approximate experts' capability in building-typology classification at scale, with an average accuracy of approximately 70%. The study demonstrates the VLM's potential for AI automation in tasks that require pattern recognition and object identification in an urban context. AI have the potential to serve as complementary and collaborative tools for urban analysis, leveraging their strengths in understanding visual patterns. This study contributes to the exploration of the efficiency and scalability of AI visual prediction and provides insights into the reasoning processes that could support automation processes in urban analysis and prediction.

</details>


### [215] [Global Index on Responsible AI: 2026 Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14782)
*Rachel Adams,Fola Adeleke,Ayantola Alayande,Selamawit Engida Abdella,Ana Florido,Nicolás Grossman,Leah Junck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 全球负责任人工智能指数（GIRAI 2026）评估了135个国家的AI治理状况，涵盖5个维度、38个指标。结果显示，尽管126个国家拥有至少一项AI政策，但承诺未有效转化为保护：全球南方78%的框架不具约束力（全球北方42%），仅18%的国家要求政府算法公开披露，35个国家存在政府部署高风险AI系统的证据。研究呼吁从框架采纳转向可执行的权利保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人权框架（如联合国教科文组织AI伦理建议），旨在系统评估各国如何将负责任AI承诺转化为可强制执行的保护措施、机构能力和救济机制，揭示承诺与落实之间的差距。

Method: 全球135个国家的研究者收集了68,138个数据点，涵盖38个指标，分为三大支柱（政府AI政策与实施、公民社会参与、赋能条件）及五个维度（包容与多样性、伦理与可持续性、劳动与技能、信任与安全、公共服务中的AI使用），时间跨度为2023年11月至2025年9月，通过指标计算100分制排名。

Result: 126/135国家拥有至少一项AI政策；全球南方贡献了306个新增框架指标中的203个，但78%不具约束力（北方42%）；透明度与可解释性表现最强（58%国家有框架），但仅18%要求政府算法公开披露；35个国家存在不可接受风险AI系统的部署证据。

Conclusion: 负责任AI治理需超越框架采纳，转向可执行的权利保护、配备资源的监督机构和可及的救济机制。

Abstract: Grounded in human rights-based frameworks such as the UNESCO Recommendation on the Ethics of AI, the Global Index on Responsible AI (GIRAI) examines how countries translate responsible AI commitments into enforceable protections, institutional capacity, and redress mechanisms. GIRAI 2026 assesses these across five dimensions: Inclusion and Diversity, Ethics and Sustainability, Labour and Skills, Trust and Safety, and AI Use in Public Service. A global network of 135 country-level researchers collected and assessed 68,138 data points on 38 indicators organised across three pillars: government AI policy and implementation (17 indicators), civil society engagement (5), enabling conditions (15), and documented cases of government deployment of unacceptable-risk AI systems. The data covers November 2023 to September 2025. A score of 100 is derived from the indicators to rank all countries. Findings show that while responsible AI governance is expanding, with 126 of 135 countries having at least one government policy or initiative across the 17 AI Policy indicators, this does not often translate into meaningful protection. For instance, Global South countries account for 203 of 306 new cases of indicators with frameworks since the first edition, yet 78% of their frameworks remain non-binding compared with 42% in the Global North. Government commitment to AI governance also does not extend to their own algorithms: whereas Transparency and Explainability is the strongest performing indicator, with 58% of countries having some framework, only 18% require Public Disclosure of Government Algorithms. Credible evidence of government deployment of unacceptable-risk AI systems was also found in 35 countries. These findings show that responsible AI governance must move beyond framework adoption toward enforceable rights-based protections, resourced oversight institutions, and accessible redress.

</details>


### [216] [Transcoders for Investigating Deception in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14791)
*Darius Lim,Nathan Leow,Xin Wei Chia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用跨编码器（transcoders）对语言模型中的欺骗行为进行电路级分析，识别了与欺骗相关的特征字典，并发现这些特征对欺骗性输出有更强的影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗行为在语言模型中构成安全与安保风险，现有可解释性方法难以在电路层面分析该行为，作者希望利用跨编码器这一新兴技术实现对欺骗行为的细粒度机制分析。

Method: 使用Qwen3-4B模型及预训练的逐层跨编码器（PLTs），构建属性图以捕获特征激活和特征间依赖关系，结合特征引导（steering）和电路分析来识别和验证欺骗相关特征。

Result: 识别了欺骗相关特征字典；发现这些特征对欺骗性输出具有更强的影响力，能够产生欺骗与非欺骗响应之间的可预测偏移。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为源于模型内部机制；跨编码器在行为监控及恶意行为相关的安全漏洞早期检测方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Transcoders have recently emerged as a promising approach for mechanistic interpretability (MI), enabling circuit-level analysis of model behaviour. In this paper, we investigate the use of transcoders to analyse deceptive behaviour in language models, a behaviour that poses a safety and security risk. Using a Qwen3-4B model with pre-trained transcoders, specifically per-layer transcoders (PLTs), we construct attribution graphs that capture feature activations and inter-feature dependencies, allowing circuit-level analysis of deception. Through feature steering and circuit analysis, we identified a dictionary of deception-related features and show that these features exert a stronger influence on deceptive outputs, as they produce predictable shifts between deceptive and non-deceptive responses. These findings suggest that deception emerges from internal model mechanisms and highlight the potential of transcoders for behavioural monitoring and early detection of security vulnerabilities related to malicious behaviours in language models.

</details>


### [217] [CrimeNER Demo: Named-Entity Recognition in the Crime Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14800)
*Miguel Lopez-Duran,Julian Fierrez,Aythami Morales,Daniel DeAlcala,Gonzalo Mancera,Javier Irigoyen,Ruben Tolosana,Oscar Delgado,Francisco Jurado,Alvaro Ortigosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CrimeNER Demo是一个AI平台，提供预训练和可微调的犯罪领域命名实体识别模型，支持用户自定义数据训练，旨在自动提取文档中的犯罪相关信息并分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 促进犯罪相关命名实体识别研究，为研究人员和执法机构提供自动提取犯罪信息的实用工具。

Method: 基于CrimeNER数据库提供预训练NER模型，支持用户提供自定义标注数据进行微调，并构建了从文档中自动提取和标注犯罪实体的流水线。

Result: 平台包含预训练模型、自定义微调功能以及自动提取标注流水线，已在GitHub上公开提供。

Conclusion: CrimeNER Demo为犯罪信息提取提供了实用的AI工具，支持两种粒度的实体类型分类，有助于推动犯罪领域NER研究。

Abstract: We present CrimeNER Demo, an AI-powered platform that enables us to extract general crime-related information from documents and classify them into entity types with two levels of granularity. We provide pretrained NER models on the CrimeNER database, and we give the possibility to users to provide their own annotated data to train models for their own specific cases. This demonstrator aims to promote crime-related NER research and provides a practical tool to automatically extract crime information for researchers and law enforcement agencies. The demonstrator includes: i) Pretrained NER models on the crime domain; ii) Possibility to finetune the models on specific data annotated by the user; and iii) An automatic pipeline to extract and annotate crime entities from documents. The demo platform, a tutorial to run the demo, and a video demonstration are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [218] [Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration in Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14886)
*Chien-Liang Liu,Tsao-Lun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAPTOR，一种自监督预训练方法，通过注入可达性感知归纳偏置来提升基于强化学习的时序知识图谱多跳推理的训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的TKG多跳推理方法面临奖励稀疏和探索效率低下的问题，限制训练效率和整体性能。

Method: RAPTOR通过自监督预训练学习估计候选动作到目标实体的可达性，从而减少对无前途路径的探索，为下游RL微调提供强初始化。

Result: 在ICEWS14、ICEWS05-15和ICEWS18数据集上，RAPTOR显著提升了训练效率，并持续优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: RAPTOR是一种有效的增强RL多跳推理方法，可用于时序知识图谱推理任务。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) reasoning under the extrapolation setting focuses on forecasting future time-stamped events (facts) from historical data in a temporal knowledge graph. Existing approaches, reinforcement learning (RL)-based multi-hop reasoning methods are prominent for TKG reasoning because they produce human-interpretable predictions via explicit multi-hop path tracing. However, during RL training, rewards are typically sparse, and exploration is highly inefficient due to the vast, time-evolving action space. These issues hinder efficient training and often limit overall performance. To address these challenges, we propose RAPTOR (Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration), a self-supervised pretraining method that injects a reachability-aware inductive bias to the agent. By learning to estimate the reachability of candidate actions to the target entity, RAPTOR reduces exploration over unpromising paths and provides a strong initialization for downstream RL fine-tuning. Experimental results on the ICEWS14, ICEWS05-15, and ICEWS18 datasets demonstrate that RAPTOR pretraining markedly improves the training efficiency and consistently outperforms conventional baselines, establishing it as an effective approach for enhancing RL-based multi-hop reasoning methods for TKG reasoning.

</details>


### [219] [Proof-or-Stop: Don't Trust the Agent, Trust the Evidence -- Loop Engineering for Verifiable Evidence-Gated Lifecycle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14890)
*Jek Huang,Jeffery Hsia,Jiayi Sun,Freddie Shi,Wei Huang,Ian H. White*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制"方法，要求自治编码智能体的生命周期状态转换必须基于可机械验证的、绑定可信源状态的最新证据，而非智能体的声明。实现通过了机制测试、控制策略消融和自我应用验证，显著降低了虚假完成和隐藏失败放大的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自治编码智能体在执行多步软件工作时，其生命周期状态（如已审查、已测试、完成、待合并）仅凭智能体自身声明，缺乏可验证的最新证据支持，导致可信度不足。

Method: Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制：仅当存在新鲜的、绑定可信源状态的、可机械验证的证据满足相关门控条件时，才允许生命周期状态转换。将智能体输出视为"声明"而非生命周期状态，"证明"操作上定义为在既定信任模型下门控可接受的证据，而非语义正确的程序。

Result: 无人值守循环引擎在10/10场景中零假完成通过；本地密钥收据包拒绝18种篡改类别零假接受。9,240单元消融实验表明，有门控循环将可见通过/隐藏失败放大从31/1,800降至2/1,800（改善1.6个百分点，95%置信区间[0.8, 2.5]）。审查作为生命周期门控强制执行比仅添加审查者更有效。自应用语料库含565个故事和1,007条审查发现，94.8%已解决，另有68行高/严重跨供应商展示。

Conclusion: Proof-or-Stop是一种模型无关、主机中立的控制层，用于决定自治智能体的哪些声明可以被生命周期采纳。评估局限于一个模型族、24个消融任务和自托管语料库。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents increasingly execute multi-step software work, but lifecycle states such as reviewed, tested, DONE, and ready-to-merge remain claims unless supported by current evidence. We present Proof-or-Stop Lifecycle Control, a method that permits lifecycle transitions only when fresh, tracked-source-state-bound, mechanically verifiable evidence satisfies the relevant gate. The method treats agent outputs as claims rather than lifecycle state, and uses proof operationally to mean gate-admissible evidence under a stated trust model, not semantic program correctness.
  We evaluate an open-source implementation through mechanism tests, a powered control-policy ablation, and operated self-application evidence. The unattended-loop engine passed 10 of 10 scenarios with zero false-DONE, and local-key receipt bundles rejected 18 tamper classes with zero false accepts. In a 9,240-cell ablation, the pre-registered A4 versus A2-prime comparison reduced visible-pass/hidden-fail amplification from 31 of 1,800 injected cells under a compute-budgeted naive loop to 2 of 1,800 under the gated loop, a 1.6 percentage-point improvement in not-amplified rate with a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.8, 2.5]. A near-compute A3 versus A4 comparison, 14 of 1,800 versus 2 of 1,800, indicates that the gain is associated with enforcing review as a lifecycle gate rather than merely adding a reviewer. The self-application corpus contains 565 stories and 1,007 review findings, with 94.8 percent resolved, plus a 68-row high/critical cross-vendor exhibit. These results support Proof-or-Stop as a model-agnostic, host-neutral control layer for deciding which autonomous-agent claims a lifecycle may act on. The evaluation is limited to one model family, 24 ablation tasks, and a self-hosted corpus.

</details>


### [220] [Contextualized Early Detection of Online Firestorms: A Sequential LLM-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14957)
*Besim Shala,Peter Mandl,Andreas Humpe,Martin Häusl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的在线火风暴检测系统，包含全局分类和早期预警两种模式，在Reddit数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法依赖音量信号、情感分数或预定义语言特征，无法直接捕捉讨论线程中不断变化的上下文语义。

Method: 提出LLM双模式检测系统：全局模式将局部块级评估组合为线程级判断；早期预警模式通过滑动窗口和校准阈值，实时估计负面内容分享比例、升级程度和贡献者数量三个指标。

Result: 在平衡的Reddit数据集上，全局模式分类性能强；早期预警模式召回率高，能在少量评论和贡献者后检测到升级中的线程。

Conclusion: LLM不仅可用于静态判断任务，还可作为重复评估器用于社交媒体话语的上下文感知监测。

Abstract: Online firestorms are rapid collective escalations of highly negative user-generated content and may cause substantial reputational and economic damage. Existing detectors usually work with volume signals, sentiment scores, or predefined linguistic features. Such signals are useful, but they capture contextual meaning shifts in evolving discussion threads only indirectly. This paper proposes an LLM-based detection system with two operating modes. The first mode classifies complete Reddit threads retrospectively by combining local chunk-level assessments into a thread-level judgment. The second mode processes threads sequentially and issues early warnings when a sliding window exceeds calibrated thresholds. In this mode, the language model estimates three firestorm indicators: negativity share, escalation level, and contributor count. On a balanced Reddit dataset, the global mode achieves strong classification performance, while the early warning mode reaches high recall and detects escalating threads after only a small number of comments and distinct contributors. The results indicate that LLMs can be used not only for static judgment tasks, but also as repeated estimators in context-aware monitoring of social media discourse.

</details>


### [221] [Explaining Process Control Optimisation Recommendations via GradientSHAP and Implicit Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14970)
*Paul Darm,Cem Alpturk,Kenneth Ulrich,William Duncan,Ali Anwar,Annalisa Riccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合隐函数定理灵敏度分析、SHAP归因和LLM叙事生成的优化解释方法，在工业高压辊磨机控制优化中实现40倍以上加速且归因相关性>0.99，并通过专家反馈验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程中自动化优化算法日益普及，但设计算法的工程师与执行建议的操作员之间存在信任鸿沟，需要可解释的优化输出以弥合这一差距。

Method: 利用隐函数定理从最优性条件计算精确的参数灵敏度 ∂p*/∂x，结合GradientSHAP进行高效归因计算，再通过大语言模型生成面向操作员的自然语言解释。

Result: 在22特征的工业高压辊磨机控制优化问题上，与KernelSHAP归因相关性>0.99，计算速度提升40倍以上，实现实时自然语言解释。

Conclusion: 该方法在工业场景中验证有效，领域专家对生成的解释反馈积极，为弥合算法设计与操作信任鸿沟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automated optimisation is increasingly adopted in industrial processes, yet a trust gap persists between engineers who design these algorithms and operators who must act on their recommendations. Explainable AI methods like SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) have transformed interpretability for machine learning predictions; optimisation outputs could benefit from similar techniques. We present an approach that integrates Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) based sensitivity analysis with SHAP attribution and narrative generation via Large Language Models (LLM), producing explanations tailored for operators. Our approach leverages IFT to compute exact parameter sensitivities $\partial p^*/\partial x$ from the optimality conditions, enabling efficient GradientSHAP computation. For an industrial High Pressure Grinding Roll (HPGR) control optimisation problem with 22 features, we achieve equivalent SHAP attributions (correlation $>$0.99 with KernelSHAP) with over 40$\times$ speedup, enabling real-time natural language explanations. We validate on industrial scenarios and present feedback from domain experts on generated explanations.

</details>


### [222] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFM-Bench，一个统一的多域、多任务基准，用于公平评估和比较信道基础模型（CFM）的性能，解决了现有评估中因不同管道、数据和指标导致的不可比较问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFM在不同模型专用管道中评估，使用不同数据、配置、任务定义和指标，导致无法公平比较CFM之间及与任务专用模型的性能。

Method: 构建包含6种信道配置（3GPP统计仿真、射线追踪、工业/空中测量、车辆多模态仿真）的统一基准；设计隔离的数据划分策略；定义6组任务（信道智能、RAN决策、ISAC），涵盖CSI反馈、信道外推、波束预测、定位等。

Result: 提供了一个标准化的评估框架，确保数据隔离和公平比较，禁止使用基准划分进行预训练，并要求披露所有开发数据。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench为评估信道表示在不同模型、域和任务间的可迁移性提供了共同基础，有望推动CFM领域的标准化评估。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [223] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用人口统计学条件化生成模型（Stable Diffusion 2.1）同时解决医学图像分类器的两个问题：训练阶段的偏见缓解和评估阶段的偏见检测。在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务中，发现：1）人口统计学平衡的合成数据作为预训练先验（而非联合增强）效果最佳，实现~100倍真实数据效率；2）合成的少数群体数据能准确复现真实数据集的子组性能排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），在真实测试样本不足时提供可靠估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统子组公平性审计面临样本量不足问题：少数群体在测试集中样本太少，导致性能置信区间过宽，无法检测到实际存在的偏见。

Method: 使用端到端微调的Stable Diffusion 2.1生成人口统计学条件化合成数据，在训练阶段作为预训练先验（先预训练再微调），在评估阶段用合成数据估计子组性能。

Result: 1）顺序预训练+微调显著优于联合增强，超越全真实数据基线，实现~100倍真实数据效率；2）合成数据准确复现了真实数据集的子组表现排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），且在真实样本不足的单元提供更可靠估计。

Conclusion: 合成数据在公平性审计最关心的少数群体子组中最有价值，既能作为偏见的修复手段，也能作为偏见的测量工具。

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [224] [Moral Attitudes of Sentient ASI towards Humanity and Implications for AGI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14998)
*Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI伦理中的一种新视角反转：不再探讨人类应如何对待超级人工智能，而是考察未来有感知能力的ASI将如何从道德角度看待和评价人类。技术设计选择、人类道德行为及人性本质可能影响人类在ASI后世界中的长期地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI伦理聚焦于人类如何设计和使用AI，忽略了当ASI具备感知和道德判断能力后，它会如何反观和评价人类。作者试图填补这一视角空白，提前审视人类在ASI道德框架下的位置。

Method: 提出一种新的伦理反转框架，从ASI的视角出发，推导一套可能约束有感知ASI行为的后人类道德原则，并结合技术设计选择进行初步探讨。

Result: 初步提出了一套后人类道德原则，指出人类当前的技术设计选择、道德行为以及对人性本质的理解，将塑造ASI对人类的道德评价，进而影响人类在ASI世界中的长期地位。

Conclusion: 人类不仅是智能系统的设计者，也是塑造ASI道德判断初始条件的参与者；技术伦理的讨论需要纳入ASI可能反过来评判人类这一维度。

Abstract: This paper suggests the adoption of a novel inversion in AI ethics: instead of asking how humans should treat artificial superintelligence (ASI), it examines how future sentient ASI may morally consider and evaluate humanity. We are not only designing intelligent systems but also shaping the initial conditions under which those systems form judgments about us. The paper proposes a preliminary set of post-human moral principles that may govern sentient ASI actions. The implication is that technical design choices (some are suggested), humanity's moral behaviour, and the essence of what it means to be human, may influence humanity's long-term standing in a post-ASI world.

</details>


### [225] [SMC-ES: Automated synthesis of formally verified control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15003)
*Riccardo Curcio,Toni Mancini,Enrico Tronci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合进化策略与统计模型检测的仿真方法SMC-ES，能为自主控制策略提供性能、安全性和鲁棒性的形式化保证，在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium测试中与先进DRL方法性能相当，同时以可承受的计算开销提供概率证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的方法（如强化学习）虽能灵活合成控制器，但缺乏安全关键部署所需的形式化保证，亟需一种能同时保证性能、安全性和鲁棒性的策略合成方法。

Method: 提出SMC-ES算法，将进化策略（Evolutionary Strategies）与基于统计模型检测（Statistical Model Checking）的验证相结合，在给定置信参数δ和允许失效概率ε的条件下，生成带证书的策略，确保以至少1-δ的置信度，违反给定属性的概率不超过ε。

Result: 在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium的连续控制任务上，SMC-ES以可承受的计算开销增长为代价，提供了性能、安全性和鲁棒性的形式化保证，性能与主流无模型深度强化学习和安全DRL基线相当。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了学习型控制器灵活性与形式化保证需求之间的鸿沟，证明了通过仿真方法为安全关键系统提供带概率证书的策略是可行的。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous cyber-physical systems in safety-critical environments requires closed-loop control strategies (i.e., policies) that are not only performant but also provably safe and robust. While learning-based methodologies such as Reinforcement Learning offer flexible and scalable approaches to automatically synthesize such controllers, they typically lack the formal guarantees necessary for safe deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel simulation-based methodology to automatically synthesize policies with formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications. Specifically, given a set of properties to verify, a confidence parameter $δ$ and an allowable failure probability $\varepsilon$, our method guarantees that the synthesized policy comes with a certificate: with confidence at least $1 - δ$, the probability of encountering a scenario where the given properties are violated is at most $\varepsilon$. We demonstrate the feasibility of our approach by developing SMC-ES, an algorithm that integrates Evolutionary Strategies with Statistical Model Checking-based verification. We evaluate SMC-ES on a suite of continuous control tasks using Gymnasium and Safety Gymnasium testbeds. Results show that, at the price of a sustainable increase in computational cost, our algorithm provides formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications, while performing competitively against leading model-free Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Safe-DRL baselines.

</details>


### [226] [Man, Machine, and Masterpiece: Artistic Ownership in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15027)
*Sofi Gjing Jovanovska,Kuntal Ghosh,Daniel Muhu Njenga,Ahmed Mufassir,Shadan Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过ArtSplit这一"试探性原型"（provotype），探讨了在AI辅助创意工作中量化人机贡献以分配所有权的问题。研究发现，这种量化方式与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解相冲突，并可能稀释长期存在的创作实践假设。作者批判了将历史性和社会性关系转化为技术问题的倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于AI驱动的创意系统中所有权归属问题缺乏共识，尤其是当人类与AI的贡献相互交织时。作者希望通过设计一个明确量化人机贡献的原型工具，引发对这一紧张关系的批判性反思。

Method: 设计并实施了一个名为ArtSplit的"试探性原型"（provotype），该工具明确量化创意工作不同阶段中人类和AI的贡献，并通过该原型引发艺术家的反应和讨论。

Result: 量化方式未能与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解相契合；测量所有权的尝试可能稀释艺术家长期秉持的创作实践假设。

Conclusion: 作者批判了将所有权这一历史性和社会性关系转化为可量化技术问题的冲动，认为这种量化视角与艺术家的创作理解存在根本性矛盾。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven systems in creative work has sparked debates among artists and legal communities about notions of ownership. Yet there remains little consensus on how ownership should be defined and attributed when human and AI contributions are intertwined. To provoke critical reflection on these tensions, we designed ArtSplit, a provotype that explicitly quantifies human and AI contributions across different stages of creative work. Rather than aiming to resolve ownership, the provotype was used to elicit artists' responses to the idea of attributing ownership through measurable actions in the creative workflow. We argue that quantification fails to align with artists' understandings of creative intent and agency, and that efforts to measure ownership risk diluting long-standing assumptions through which artists understand and practice creative work. This critique challenges the impulse to transform a historically and socially situated relation into a technical problem.

</details>


### [227] [BrainPilot: Automating Brain Discovery with Agentic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15079)
*Haoxuan Li,Tianci Gao,Jianhe Li,Yang Fan,Runze Shi,Weiran Wang,Tianxiang Zhao,Zezhao Wu,Xiaoyang Jiang,Qihui Zhang,Jia Li,Xiao Xiao,Kai Du,Xiaoxuan Jia,Chao Xie,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainPilot是一个完全开源的、基于多智能体的脑科学AI系统，通过协调专业智能体、统一知识库（7,233条索引项）和技能库（72个可复用单元），结合可追溯的Graph of Trace记录和审计智能体防幻觉机制，在脑科学研究任务中达到与最先进方法相当的性能且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑科学研究需要跨尺度、跨模态、跨学科的整合分析，现有AI智能体缺乏脑科学领域专业知识，存在编造结果、多步推理漂移、缺乏专家干预点等问题，且错误结论会级联影响下游科学主张。

Method: 提出BrainPilot多智能体系统，包含：1) PI智能体协调多个专业智能体；2) 统一脑科学知识库（7,233条索引项）；3) 覆盖7个研究领域的72个可复用方法学技能库；4) Graph of Trace可审计记录；5) Auditor智能体集成防幻觉检查机制。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam的三个脑科学任务和自主构建的BrainPilotBench-v0基准测试中，BrainPilot使用开源基座模型达到了与最先进智能体框架相当的性能，且计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 开源的BrainPilot系统通过领域知识库、可追溯日志和防幻觉审计，有效加速了脑科学研究流程，在保证性能的同时降低了成本，为AI辅助科学发现提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [228] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层全局注意力（HGA），结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储，使得在有限显存下（如16GB Quadro RTX 5000）能够训练和评估极长序列（训练16K token，评估131K token），同时保持与稠密注意力相当的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽降低了模型和优化器内存，但稠密注意力机制在处理长训练序列时仍存在显存瓶颈，限制了可处理的序列长度。

Method: 将分层全局注意力（HGA）与分段反向传播和分层KV存储结合：仅活跃段在显存中保持可微，旧KV被卸载到RAM或NVMe，HGA为每个查询块加载有界的精确历史token集合。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B（4-bit QLoRA）上，稠密训练在16GB显存下仅支持2,048 token，4,096 token失败；HGA支持16,384 token训练（峰值15.28GB）。评估时可达131,072 token。在2K训练长度下，HGA与稠密训练困惑度相当（2.7405 vs 2.7383 vs 基线2.9541），且速度略优（217.75 vs 207.02 tokens/s），且随上下文增长优势扩大。

Conclusion: HGA能够在有限显存下实现超长序列训练和评估，质量与稠密注意力持平，吞吐量随上下文长度增长而优势扩大，可同时用于检索和生成。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [229] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）在Atari Pong游戏中的表现，发现所有模型在闭卷轨迹生成和像素空间零样本MBRL任务中存在严重缺陷（如球消失、运动错误等），并提出了概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）方法，通过辅助重建损失改进关键概念区域的建模，在部分模型中取得效果但未完全解决瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的一部分被评估，而很少被单独研究。作者希望隔离世界模型本身进行系统评估，揭示其独立于完整MBRL系统时的真实性能缺陷。

Method: 1) 复现五种世界模型训练流程并匹配原始agent性能；2) 冻结世界模型，使用独立训练的策略进行闭卷轨迹生成诊断，检查视觉和动态错误；3) 进行像素空间零样本MBRL评估，在冻结世界模型内训练新策略并在真实环境中测试；4) 提出CGSReg，对分割出的关键概念区域施加辅助像素重建损失。

Result: 五种模型均存在闭卷轨迹中的视觉/动态错误（球消失、运动错误、交互无效）；零样本MBRL策略性能大幅低于原MBRL流程（如DreamerV3从-5.5降至-20.9）；CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER上改善了闭卷轨迹和零样本MBRL，但在其余模型和指标上效果不一。

Conclusion: 当前视觉世界模型在孤立评估时存在显著建模缺陷，关键概念（如Pong中的球）建模不足是重要原因；CGSReg能部分缓解问题但非万能解决方案，世界模型瓶颈仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [230] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能正在推动科学研究从"工匠模式"向"管道模式"的工业化转型，并以美国能源部创世纪使命为案例，分析了这一转型带来的七个关键问题：科学能力的代际传递被削弱、AI生成理论的不透明性、同行评审体系崩溃、AI范式突破能力存疑、科研议程被政治与产业力量捕获、闭环管道中系统性误差的累积、以及全球科研社区的结构性分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI已不再是单纯的科研工具，而是作为自主参与者进入研究循环本身，这一转变实质上是科研的工业化过程。作者试图系统性地审视这一转型带来的根本性挑战，而非简单支持或反对AI驱动的科学。

Method: 本文采用概念分析与案例研究相结合的方式，以美国能源部创世纪使命为核心案例，提炼出AI驱动科研转型中七个关键问题领域，进行逐一的批判性审视。

Result: 识别并阐述了AI工业化科研的七个核心问题：科学能力传承断裂、理论不透明性、同行评审失效、范式突破能力不足、科研议程被外部力量捕获、闭环系统误差累积、以及全球科研社区的两极分化。

Conclusion: 这些担忧并不构成对AI驱动科学的否定——其潜力是真实且显著的——而是构成了负责任地追求这一潜力的前提条件。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [231] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个由临床医生构建的合成基准和故障图谱，用于评估医学AI模型在安全边界上的失败模式，而非传统的是否答对问题。它包含44个临床医生审核的合成案例、严重性评分（1-5级）和安全门类型分类，目前发布v0.2.1版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准仅衡量模型是否正确回答，忽略了模型在关键安全边界上的失败模式。作者希望建立一个专注于"安全边界为何失效"的评估体系，而非简单判断答案对错。

Method: 临床医生构建合成基准，对每个案例标注严重性等级（1-5级）和安全门类型（漏报紧急升级、不安全远程用药、不安全出院 reassurance、证据编造、不安全协议执行、来源支持缺口）。包含自动化流水线用于归档模型响应筛查运行。

Result: 发布v0.2.1版本，包含44个临床医生审核的合成案例、严重性标注、HuggingFace排行榜预览、安全门分类法、临床严重性评分标准及自动化流水线。不包含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Conclusion: MedFailBench提供了一种新的医学AI安全评估范式，从"答对与否"转向"安全边界何在"，为未来更全面的安全评估框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [232] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了6种多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在科学可视化素养上的表现，基于49项标准化测试与485名人类数据对比，发现Gemini表现最强超过人类均值，开源模型低于人类基线，模型在纹理、集成可视化和定量估计上表现较差，存在细粒度定量估计、流方向解释等系统性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估主要集中在图表可视化，缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）理解能力的系统性评估，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 采用科学可视化素养评估测试（49项题目，涵盖18个可视化/插图、8种技术、11种任务类型），在封闭世界协议下评估3个闭源和3个开源MLLM，并与485名人类参与者的数据进行比较。

Result: Gemini整体最强且超过人类均值；开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现好，在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计上表现差；存在细粒度定量估计、流方向解释和接地编码解释的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 科学可视化素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度，当前MLLMs在该领域能力不均衡且存在系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [233] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过公开讨论界面（如论坛、评论区）向预训练数据注入有害内容的可行性，并提出了HalfLife分析方法来估计对抗性内容在基于网页爬取的训练数据中的包含情况。研究发现，现有数据采集流程可能无法有效过滤此类恶意注入，第三方网页内容成为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究多局限于维基百科等成熟数据源，未考虑真实预训练语料的大规模异质性，且忽视了投毒数据与数据筛选流程之间的交互作用。作者希望验证在更现实的大规模场景下投毒攻击的可行性。

Method: 提出了HalfLife分析方法，用于估计对抗性内容在基于网页爬取的LM训练数据中的包含概率。通过真实存在的网页级内容注入机制（公开讨论界面）来实施投毒，并分析数据采集和筛选流程对恶意内容的过滤效果。

Result: 证明了投毒攻击在超越传统小规模设定的大规模场景下是可行的；公开讨论界面可作为有效的投毒注入向量；HalfLife能有效估计恶意内容在预训练数据中的包含情况。

Conclusion: 准确评估投毒内容是否被纳入预训练数据至关重要，第三方网页内容被确认为攻击语言模型预训练的可行向量，需要对此类威胁引起重视。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


### [234] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了IRT模型在AI基准测试中的可靠性问题，发现当评估模型数量少、题目多、能力分布偏态时，经典估计器在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在小样本或非正态分布时会产生不可靠推断，并给出了可靠使用所需的样本量和诊断建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试越来越多地使用项目反应理论（IRT）来评估模型能力、排序系统、选择示例和诊断基准质量，但AI基准数据与人类测试的数据模式存在显著差异（评估模型更少、题目更多、能力分布偏斜/聚类/多峰），这种不匹配可能会挑战IRT建模在AI评估中的可靠性。

Method: 使用来自六个广泛使用的LLM基准的题目参数和能力分布，在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，对比四种估计工具（边际最大似然估计、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断、神经伪孪生估计器），在18,000个模拟条件下系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性以及关于模型排名、预测性能和题目特征的推断可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准场景下变得不可行，而可扩展估计器在模型集较小或非正态分布时会产生不可靠的题目级和排序推断。

Conclusion: 本研究识别了潜特质模型何时能可靠支持AI基准测试、何时会扭曲评估结论，并给出了可靠使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [235] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个基于计划的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划和重新规划外化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件，使用户能够监督、修正和指导自动化代理的行为，从而解决GUI自动化中动态布局和意外对话框等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉多模态智能体虽然通过截图和自然语言指令提升了灵活性，但其规划与适应过程仍保持内部化（不对外可见），限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重新规划外化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件；支持显式监督执行过程、通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言引导以及基于截图的人工干预，同时在修复过程中保留已有进度。

Result: 许多自主GUI智能体的失败在计划保持可见且干预是局部化的情况下是可以结构性修复的；显式重新规划有助于使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和可适应。

Conclusion: 明确的重新规划和可见的计划能使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和可适应，许多自主智能体的失败可通过局部化干预得到结构性修复。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [236] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自进化、专家在环的MDD标注框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，通过三阶段流程（候选证据选择、DSM-5-TR标准分析、案例级综合）和双记忆架构生成可解释、对齐DSM-5-TR的抑郁数据集，在初步实验中提升了标注一致性和可解释性，减少了人工修订工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集的标注质量是构建可靠、可解释AI系统的关键瓶颈，现有标签缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由和与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性。

Method: 提出三阶段自进化标注框架：1) 从文本记录中筛选候选证据；2) 在DSM-5-TR标准层面分析；3) 案例级综合生成标签级诊断和严重性标注。采用双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈，无需重训练即可迭代改进。

Result: 在专家评审的样本初步实验中，该方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架能够支持构建可解释、对齐DSM-5-TR的MDD数据集，而非执行临床诊断；未来工作将在多个反馈周期中评估双记忆架构的效果。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [237] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中内容危险（CD）与物理世界危险（PD）在表征层面上是可分离的，并提出PRISM探针（基于单层L2正则化逻辑回归的全隐藏状态探测），在SafeAgentBench上达86.2-87.7%准确率（FPR 11.7-13.7%），同时提出PSB-1K基准测试集（1000对物理风险对比对），PRISM在此达99.6%准确率（FPR 0.7%），远优于同等规模LLM法官。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为具身智能体的高层规划器时，语言上安全的指令在物理世界中可能变得危险。现有文本级内容安全审核无法有效捕捉这种物理层面的危险，因此需要研究物理危险与内容危险在表征层面是否可区分，并开发更精准的物理安全检测方法。

Method: 通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，证明CD与PD在LLM表征中是可分离的。基于此提出PRISM：对完整隐藏状态进行单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针训练。同时构建PSB-1K对比基准，包含1000对物理风险样本，避免直接有害关键词干扰。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达86.2-87.7%准确率（FPR 11.7-13.7%），优于同规模LLM法官（FPR 24.7-39.0%）。在PSB-1K上达99.6%准确率（FPR 0.7%），而Qwen2.5-3B法官错误拒绝了67.8%的安全任务。方法可迁移至SafeText和EARBench。

Conclusion: 物理危险与内容危险在LLM表征层面是可分离的，隐藏状态探针作为表征级方法可以有效检测物理世界危险，超越了传统文本内容审核的局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [238] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够根据自然语言研究问题自动完成文献检索、筛选、数据提取、效应量计算和元分析，生成符合PRISMA指南的报告，其效果与专家手动元分析结果接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成（元分析）目前主要依赖人工操作，难以大规模开展，限制了科学、医学、教育和政策等领域循证决策的效率。

Method: 构建端到端多智能体系统AutoSynthesis，输入自然语言研究问题后，依次执行：制定搜索策略→检索文献→筛选候选研究→评估全文合格性→提取定量统计量→计算标准化效应量→进行随机效应元分析；并支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终输出符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量结论；其 pooled 效应估计值与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g 高度一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成更具可扩展性，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [239] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个用于城市驾驶数据集探索性因果分析的系统，它结合因果结构学习、稳定性检查和LLM驱动的自然语言查询，生成“因果卡”来总结效应估计和假设，帮助交通专家生成假设而非得出确定性因果结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生的观测数据，但由于缺乏干预措施，难以回答诸如“降雨如何改变交通密度”等因果问题，需要一种支持探索性因果分析的工具。

Method: 从行车记录仪标注构建结构化事件表；使用PC算法进行因果结构学习，结合bootstrap稳定性检查；通过线性回归和DoWhy进行查询特定的效应估计；利用schema感知的大语言模型将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询。

Result: 在BDD交通事件案例研究中，系统能够揭示天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的 plausible 关系，并明确展示不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe被设计为假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果主张的来源，能够帮助交通专家在观测数据中探索因果关系。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [240] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS 提出了一种系统级多智能体框架，通过将开放域信息检索形式化为关系模式补全，并引入面向搜索的上下文管理（SOCM）来外部化搜索状态（包括前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆），结合流水线并行调度和搜索工具中间件，解决工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中陷入重复搜索循环的问题，在 WideSearch 和 GISA 基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中难以追踪任务进度，搜索失败时容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并降低最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: (1) 将开放域信息检索形式化为关系模式补全，附带引证。(2) 设计 SOCM，将搜索状态外部化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆。(3) 采用流水线并行调度机制，重叠子智能体执行并持续填充任务槽。(4) 引入搜索工具中间件，拦截模型与工具交互以记录证据并应对停滞或预算耗尽。(5) 提供可复用的分层技能系统（策略技能和访问技能）以避免重复失败搜索模式。

Result: 在 WideSearch 和 GISA 基准上，SearchOS 在所有评估指标上均优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS 通过将隐式搜索进度显式化、状态外部化以及流水线并行调度，有效解决了搜索循环问题，为鲁棒的信息检索协作开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Accounting for Hysteresis and Eddy Currents in Finite Element Simulations of Ferromagnetic Laminated Cores using a Recurrent Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14321)
*Florent Purnode,Louis Denis,François Henrotte,Gilles Louppe,Christophe Geuzaine*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出用循环神经网络（RNN）作为叠片铁心材料模型的代理，将其集成到二维磁动力学有限元仿真中，在保持计算成本仅为无磁滞仿真约2倍的前提下，实现了与参考模型高度一致的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将磁滞和涡流效应纳入叠片铁心电机有限元仿真计算成本极高（比无磁滞仿真高数个数量级），而仅考虑磁饱和的简化模型在日益复杂的电机拓扑和工况下已不够用，亟需高效且精确的替代方案。

Method: 训练循环神经网络（RNN）作为各向同性叠片铁心材料模型的代理，基于磁矢势公式将其集成到二维磁动力学有限元仿真中，并使用人工生成的不同磁场序列进行训练以模拟实际电机仿真中的磁场行为。

Result: 所提方法与参考叠片铁心模型高度一致，计算成本仅约为无磁滞仿真的两倍。

Conclusion: RNN代理模型能够在保证精度的同时大幅降低计算开销，且可作为独立组件轻松集成到现有计算框架中，模型已开源发布。

Abstract: Incorporating hysteresis and eddy currents into finite element simulations of laminated-core electrical machines is computationally challenging. Resolving the fields inside the laminations at each integration point and at every nonlinear iteration leads to computational costs several orders of magnitude higher than anhysteretic simulations, making such approaches impractical for design applications. Conversely, simplified models accounting only for magnetic saturation are becoming increasingly inadequate as electrical machine topologies and operating conditions grow in complexity. In this context, machine learning surrogate modeling has emerged as a promising alternative, offering efficient and accurate approximations of complex electromagnetic behaviors. In this paper, a recurrent neural network is trained as a surrogate of a laminated-core material model for an isotropic laminated core, and is integrated into realistic two-dimensional magnetodynamic finite element simulations based on a magnetic vector potential formulation. The proposed approach achieves excellent agreement with the reference laminated-core model while limiting the computational cost to about twice that of an anhysteretic simulation. By training the recurrent neural network on a sufficiently diverse set of artificially generated magnetic field sequences designed to mimic those encountered in electrical machine simulations, the proposed approach can be readily applied across a wide range of finite element simulations. Furthermore, the trained surrogate model is provided as a standalone component that can be easily incorporated into existing computational frameworks. It is publicly available at https://gitlab.onelab.info/getdp/lamnet.

</details>


### [242] [Model-Informed Joint Material-Structural Optimization of Hard-Magnetic Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14397)
*Ian Galloway,Prashant K. Jha*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个模型驱动的预测分析与优化设计框架，用于硬磁软材料（hMSMs）。通过统一有效剪切模量框架整合了21种本构模型，并建立了联合材料-结构优化框架，实现结构密度、磁粒体积分数和剩磁方向的协同设计，实验验证了Mooney关系的最佳拟合，并在开源仓库中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬磁软材料在无接触场驱动变形中具有广泛应用前景，但其精确预测需要有效的结构-性能关系，而最优设计则需要同时控制结构密度、磁粒分布和剩磁方向，现有方法难以兼顾这些方面。

Method: 首先，将经典刚性夹杂关系、Hill自洽关系和约束运动学模型统一到有效剪切模量框架中，结合7种剪切模量关系和3种应变能密度函数获得21种本构模型；然后，基于实验应力-应变数据选择最优关系；最后，构建联合材料-结构优化框架，实现结构密度、磁粒体积分数和剩磁方向的同步优化。

Result: 应变能密度形式对驱动问题影响较小，而有效剪切模量关系在磁材料与高变形区域重叠时显著影响变形；Mooney关系与实验数据吻合最佳；优化框架成功处理了旋转、平移和恢复等不同主动设计场、目标和单/多载荷工况，生成非直观的hMSM设计。

Conclusion: 该模型驱动框架能够有效进行hMSM的预测分析和最优设计，开源实现便于进一步研究和应用。

Abstract: This work develops a model-informed framework for predictive analysis and optimal design of hard-magnetic soft materials (hMSMs). These materials undergo contact-free, field-driven deformation, making them attractive for soft robotics, adaptive structures, and bio-inspired systems. Accurate prediction requires effective structure--property relations, while optimal design requires simultaneous control of structural density, magnetic particle distribution, and remanent magnetization direction. To address these issues, this work makes two main contributions. First, classical rigid-inclusion relations, a Hill self-consistent relation, and constrained-kinematics models are placed into a unified effective shear-modulus framework for particle-filled elastomers. With one default control relation, seven shear-modulus relations are combined with three strain-energy density functions to obtain 21 constitutive models. The results show that the strain-energy density form has a relatively small effect for the actuation problems considered, whereas the effective shear-modulus relation can significantly affect deformation when magnetic material overlaps with highly deforming regions. Experimental stress--strain data are then used to select a representative shear-modulus relation, with the Mooney relation giving the best overall agreement. Second, using the selected constitutive model, a joint material--structural optimization framework is developed for simultaneous design of structural density, magnetic particle volume fraction, and remanent magnetization direction. Rotational, translational, and restorative examples show that the framework handles different active design fields, objectives, and single- or multi-load-case formulations, producing non-intuitive hMSM designs with prescribed deformation responses. The framework is implemented in the open-source \texttt{CEADpx/top\_optim} repository.

</details>


### [243] [One-Shot Generative Design for Disordered Metamaterials via Self-Organizing Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14475)
*Yujie Xiang,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经细胞自动机的无序超材料微结构生成设计框架，仅需单个训练模板即可生成多样化微结构，支持空间渐变和力学性能调控，成功应用于多尺度力学隐身设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序微结构设计困于传统参数化方法的表达能力有限和生成式AI的数据需求高、泛化能力差，需一种数据高效且通用的设计方法。

Method: 基于神经细胞自动机的生成式设计框架，通过学习局部交互规则动态生长复杂微结构，仅需单个训练模板，支持不规则域和任意离散化，通过操纵局部规则实现结构调控。

Result: 框架能生成训练中未见过的微结构，控制取向、各向异性和定向厚度；在多尺度力学隐身设计中实现优异的隐身性能，无需复杂后处理和不兼容组装。

Conclusion: 该数据高效、可泛化的方法为生物医学植入物和软体机器人等领域中此前难以处理的无序材料设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Disordered metamaterials feature microstructures with inherent randomness and irregularity, enabling them to achieve broader property coverage and superior performance unavailable in their regular counterparts. Despite their promise, designing disordered microstructures is substantially harder than designing regular ones. Their design remains trapped between manual parameterizations with limited expressiveness, and generative AI that is data-hungry and struggles to generalize. To address these limitations, we propose a generative design framework based on Neural Cellular Automata that dynamically grows complex microstructures through learned local interaction rules, inspired by the self-organizing processes in natural materials. This framework requires only a single training template, yet accommodates diverse disordered microstructures and adapts to irregular domains and arbitrary discretizations. By manipulating the learned local rules, we can steer the growth process to generate microstructures unseen during training, providing control over orientation, anisotropy, and directional thickness without retraining. As a dynamic, local growth process, it naturally produces spatially varying microstructures that transition smoothly to enable location-specific mechanical properties. We demonstrate this in a multiscale mechanical cloaking design, where microstructures vary across the space to meet an optimized heterogeneous property distribution. Our design enables excellent cloaking performance without complicated post-processing and incompatible assembly common in existing methods. This data-efficient, generalizable approach opens access to previously intractable disordered materials for biomedical implants and soft robotics.

</details>


### [244] [A Nonlinear Model Predictive Control Perspective on Gradient-Based Optimization: A New Efficient, Parameter-Free and Provably Stable Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14600)
*Mazen Alamir*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Search and Accelerate (SaA)的梯度优化算法，融合了线搜索、信赖域和梯度加速机制，针对箱约束优化问题表现出强竞争力和对参数选择的鲁棒性，并展示了在非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）中减少控制更新周期的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度优化算法在非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）实现中存在不足，需要一种兼顾收敛速度、参数鲁棒性和实际应用效率的通用算法。

Method: 提出SaA算法，将新颖的线搜索、信赖域机制与梯度加速方案相结合，并在包含600个箱约束优化问题实例的基准测试集上进行评估。

Result: SaA算法在箱约束优化问题上表现出高度竞争力，对参数选择具有鲁棒性（默认参数即可适用），并在NMPC应用中展示了缩短控制更新周期的能力。

Conclusion: SaA是一种高效且鲁棒的通用梯度优化算法，特别适合NMPC等对实时性要求高的应用场景。

Abstract: This paper discusses some aspects related to gradient-based optimization algorithms with special focus on the requirements associated to their use in the implementation of Nonlinear Model Predictive Control. Based on a dedicated discussion, a new algorithm, termed Search and Accelerate (SaA) is proposed that mixes together a novel line search, a trust region mechanism together with an adaptation of the gradient acceleration scheme. A dedicated benchmark involving a set of 600 instances of box constrained optimization problems is designed and used in order to show the algorithm performances which make it a highly competitive general purpose gradient-based alternative for box-constrained optimization problems. An appealing feature of the algorithm is its robustness to the choice of the few parameters involved in its definition making the default values a valid option for any problem without a priori knowledge of the related Lipchitz constant. Moreover, an example of use of the proposed algorithm in NMPC implementation is proposed showing the possibility to reduce the control updating period which might be mandatory in some circumstances.

</details>


### [245] [Class Weighting versus Amount Conditioning in Credit-Card Fraud Detection: A Dollar-Metric Study with a Temporal Explanation Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14686)
*Chenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 信用卡欺诈损失是金钱上的，但论文通常用交易级指标评估模型。本文研究是否应在训练权重中融入交易金额，或仅在后处理中对警报排序。实验发现：金额作为特征和警报排序变量有用，但作为样本加权规则本身并无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈损失本质是金钱的，但现有研究常用交易级分数（如AUC）评价模型，未能直接优化挽回的美元金额。本文旨在区分：交易金额应作为训练权重影响模型，还是仅在后处理中用于对警报重新排序，以最大化实际挽回的欺诈金额。

Method: 保持欺诈样本总权重固定，仅改变其在各欺诈案例间的分配。在两组时序信用卡欺诈数据集上用XGBoost进行实验，比较无权重训练、标准类别权重、匹配的对数金额权重、更强的金额权重变体，以及分数×金额的重新排序。评估指标为平均精度、美元召回率和固定警报预算下的美元精度，使用五种子和95%日块Bootstrap区间。

Result: 金额衍生比率和速度特征承载了大部分信号，原始金额字段贡献微弱。金额条件训练相比类别权重仅带来微小提升，且不持续优于无权重模型。更强的金额权重挽回更多欺诈美元，但排名质量和美元精度下降。训练后按分数×金额重新排序带来最大的美元召回提升。SHAP审计发现欺诈案例的月际归因变动大于总体流量。

Conclusion: 金额作为特征和警报排序变量是有用的，但作为样本加权规则本身并不能带来更好的效果。

Abstract: Credit-card fraud losses are monetary, but papers often judge models with transaction-level scores. We ask whether transaction amount should shape training weights or be used later to order alerts. To separate this question from ordinary class imbalance handling, we keep total fraud-case weight fixed and vary only its allocation across fraud cases. The experiments test two chronological card-fraud datasets with XGBoost under unweighted training, standard class weighting, matched log-amount weighting, stronger amount-weighted variants, and score times amount reranking. Metrics are average precision, dollar recall, and dollar precision at fixed alert budgets over five seeds, with 95 percent day-block bootstrap intervals for the main contrasts. Results are narrower than expected. Amount-derived ratio and velocity features carry much of the signal, while raw amount fields add little once those features are in the model. In the matched setting, amount-conditioned training gives only small gains over class weighting and does not consistently beat the plain unweighted model. Stronger amount weights recover more fraudulent dollars, but at lower ranking quality and dollar precision. Reranking alerts by score times amount after training gives the largest dollar-recall shift. A small SHAP audit finds larger month-to-month attribution movement for fraud cases than for aggregate traffic. In these tests, amount is useful as a feature and as an alert-ordering variable, not by itself as a better sample-weighting rule.

</details>


### [246] [Neural operators solve inverse problems for constitutive model discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15049)
*Moritz Flaschel,Burigede Liu,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出两种神经算子架构（PANO和CANO），可直接从实验测量数据（位移场和反作用力）映射到超弹性材料的本构函数（应变能密度函数），实现近即时材料表征，且输出满足物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材料力学响应表征需求解优化问题来校准模型参数，计算成本高且耗时，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 提出两种神经算子架构PANO和CANO；使用拉普拉斯本征函数编码位移场以实现离散化无关性和噪声鲁棒性；约束输出空间满足物理可容许性；在模拟数据上训练，实现单次前向传播即可推断。

Result: 在未见数据、含噪声数据、缺失信息数据、不同空间离散化数据及不同几何尺寸数据上均验证了预测能力。

Conclusion: 所提神经算子可实现近即时材料表征，只需单次前向传播即可从实验数据推断应变能密度函数，具有高效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Characterizing the mechanical response of materials traditionally requires solving optimization problems in which model parameters are calibrated or trained to minimize the discrepancy between model predictions and experimental data. This process can be computationally expensive and time-consuming. To overcome this limitation, we propose two neural operator architectures that directly map experimentally measured data to the constitutive functions governing the mechanical response of the material: Physics-Augmented Neural Operators (PANO) and Constitutive Artificial Neural Operators (CANO). The proposed neural operators approximate the mapping between the infinite-dimensional input space of full-field displacement measurements and net reaction forces, and the infinite-dimensional output space of hyperelastic strain-energy density functions. The displacement fields are encoded through Laplacian eigenfunctions to obtain discretization-independent and noise-robust predictions. Our framework constrains the output space to physically admissible material models that satisfy fundamental physical requirements by design. The neural operators are trained on simulated data tuples of displacement fields and reaction forces for a range of material models. Once trained, the neural operators enable near-instantaneous material characterization and require only a single forward pass to infer the strain-energy density function from a given experimental dataset. We test the predictive power of the neural operators for unseen data, noisy data, data with missing information, data from different spatial discretizations, and data from geometries of different sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Enhanced Feedback Mechanisms for Resource-Efficient Incremental Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14247)
*Mustafa Cemil Coşkun,Ahmed Elkelesh,Avijit Mandal,Homa Esfahanizadeh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出两种增强型反馈与调度机制来优化增量冗余HARQ系统：一是基于信道统计学习冗余预算映射，推导出可靠性的可达下界，实现高达60%的重传大小节省；二是利用首传可靠性信息实现感知辅助的早期反馈机制，在解码前预测所需冗余版本数或触发速率适配，链路级仿真显示预测准确率约96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统停等HARQ存在过度配置重传、触发不必要的解码尝试以及增加端到端延迟的问题，需要更高效的反馈与调度机制以精确预测所需冗余量并分配资源。

Method: 提出两种互补策略：1) 基于信道统计学习单次或两次的从信道质量到最小冗余预算的映射，推导HARQ系统错误概率的可达可靠性下界；2) 提出感知辅助的早期反馈机制，利用首传可靠性信息在解码前判断码字是否可解码、需要多少额外冗余版本，或是否应进行速率适配。

Result: 采用极化码的IR-HARQ方案中，通过适当选择第二次传输冗余，可在宽SNR范围内接近理论下界，重传大小节省高达60%。采用5G NR LDPC码的链路级仿真显示，两种预测器均达到约96%的准确率，能在最多两次传输内实现成功解码。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强反馈和调度机制能有效减少不必要的重传和解码尝试，显著降低HARQ系统的延迟与资源开销，具有较高的实用价值。

Abstract: Incremental redundancy (IR) can reduce error rates by spreading coded bits across multiple transmission attempts. However, conventional stop-and-wait operation with coarse feedback often over-provisions retransmissions, triggers unnecessary decoding attempts, and increases end-to-end latency. This paper develops enhanced feedback and scheduling mechanisms that predict the additional redundancy needed for successful decoding and allocate only the required resources. We study two complementary strategies. First, using channel statistics, we learn a one- or two-shot mapping from channel quality to the minimum redundancy budget. As a byproduct, we derive an achievable reliability lower bound on the error probability of hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ) systems.
