Numerical results with polar-coded IR-HARQ scheme show that the bound can be closely approached by appropriately selecting the second-transmission redundancy over a wide SNR range with savings up to 60\% in retransmission size. Second, we propose a realization-aware early-feedback mechanism that uses first-transmission reliability information to make per-codeword decisions before decoding: whether the codeword is already decodable, if not, how many additional redundancy versions are needed, or whether decoding is unlikely and rate adaptation is preferable. Link-level simulations with 5G NR LDPC codes show that both predictors achieve high accuracy (about 96\% in our study), increasing the probability of successful decoding within at most two transmission occasions.

</details>


### [248] [Capacity of Uniform Noise Channels Under Average Input Power Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14352)
*Yihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了均匀噪声信道的容量及最优输入输出分布，通过发现输出密度的周期化恒等式并应用傅里叶分析技术，解决了长期悬而未决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性噪声信道的容量虽可表述为互信息最大化问题，但除高斯分布外，即使对于简单噪声分布（如均匀分布），其容量定量理解仍严重缺乏。本文旨在解决均匀噪声信道容量的长期未决问题。

Method: 关键发现是均匀噪声信道输出密度的周期化恒等式，据此应用傅里叶分析技术来精确刻画信道容量及最优输入输出分布。

Result: 精确表征了均匀噪声信道的容量，以及对应的容量可达输入和输出分布。

Conclusion: 解决了均匀噪声信道容量这一长期开放问题，为理解非高斯加性噪声信道容量提供了重要突破。

Abstract: The foundational work of Shannon (1948) identified the capacity of an additive noise channel under an average input power constraint as a mutual information maximization problem over input densities subject to a second moment constraint. However, a quantitative understanding of the channel capacity is significantly lacking even for very simple noise distributions beyond Gaussians. In particular, it is a long standing question to determine the capacity of channels with noise uniformly distributed over a centered interval. This paper settles this question by precisely characterizing the capacity and the corresponding capacity achieving input and output distributions of such channels. A key observation en route to these results is a certain periodization identity for the output density of a uniform noise channel which in turn allows for applications of Fourier analytic techniques.

</details>


### [249] [Decision Making Needs Uncertainty Quantification [Lecture Notes]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14407)
*Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，在统一决策理论框架下，系统阐述了智能体的目标与知识如何决定不确定性表示形式的选择，以实现最优决策。对于已知环境分布，风险中性智能体需后验分布，风险厌恶智能体可使用预测集和最坏情况决策；对于未知环境，提出了校准、credal集与分布鲁棒优化、贝叶斯推断三种互补方法，强调不确定性表示必须匹配决策目标与知识特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当决定智能体行动的状态变量存在不确定性时，不确定性的表示方式直接决定了智能体的性能优劣及其可信度。现有方法缺乏一个统一的决策理论视角来阐明"什么形式的不确定性表示对于什么目标和什么知识水平是充分且必要的"，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用从第一性原理出发的决策理论推导方法：1）在已知环境分布假设下，分别推导风险中性与风险厌恶智能体的最优不确定性表示（后验分布 vs 预测集+最坏情况决策）；2）在环境未知情况下，提出三种互补方法（校准、credal集+分布鲁棒优化、贝叶斯推断）处理认知不确定性，并以保证实际效用为核心统一框架。

Result: 建立了智能体目标/知识水平与不确定性表示形式之间的精确对应关系：风险中性→后验分布（充分必要），风险厌恶→预测集+最坏情况决策（无最优性损失）；面对未知环境时，三种方法各有侧重但共同服务于"匹配决策目标与知识特征+效用保证"这一核心原则。

Conclusion: 可靠决策要求不确定性表示必须匹配决策目标与智能体的知识特征，同时需要提供能够认证智能体实际获得效用的保证。这一框架为信号处理系统中的不确定性量化提供了理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: Many signal processing systems ultimately exist to {act}. Whenever the state variable that determines the action to be taken by a decision maker, or agent, is uncertain, the way that uncertainty is represented decides how well the agent performs and how much its performance can be trusted. This lecture note develops, from first principles and within a single decision-theoretic setting, the link between the {objective} and the knowledge of an agent and the form of uncertainty representation that is sufficient to act optimally. To start, assuming a known environment distribution, we show that a risk-neutral agent needs the posterior distribution over the state, whereas a risk-averse agent can rely without loss of optimality on a {prediction set} and a worst-case decision rule. We then turn to the case in which the environment is unknown, and identify three complementary approaches to address the resulting epistemic uncertainty: calibration of a fixed predictor, credal (ambiguity) sets with distributionally robust optimization, and Bayesian inference over model parameters. The common thread is that reliable decisions require an uncertainty representation matched to the decision objective and to the knowledge profile of the agent, together with a guarantee that certifies the utility the agent will actually obtain.

</details>


### [250] [Group Testing with Selectable Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14448)
*Trung-Khang Tran,Daniel McMorrow,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了群体检测问题，引入了一种可选择性阈值的检测模型，分析了在不同阈值范围下实现高概率恢复所需的测试次数，并证明了在特定条件下测试效率可趋近理论最大值1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群体检测中测试阈值通常是固定的，本文试图探索当测试阈值可以灵活选择时，能否提升检测效率，即在更少的测试次数下实现高概率的缺陷项识别。

Method: 引入可选择性阈值模型，每个测试可关联一个可选的阈值，测试结果仅当缺陷项数量不低于该阈值时为1。针对大或无界最大阈值情形推导高概率恢复的条件；针对固定最大阈值情形，提出简单高效解码方法并建立可达测试数及逆命题。

Result: 在大或无界最大阈值下，检测速率可趋近最大值1；在固定最大阈值下，在密集极限（θ→1, k=Θ(n^θ)）时可达测试数与理论下界一致。

Conclusion: 可选择性阈值模型能够有效提升群体检测的效率，在不同阈值约束下均能获得接近最优的检测性能，且固定阈值情形下提出的解码方法兼具简单性和计算效率。

Abstract: We consider the problem of group testing, in which one seeks to identify a subset of defective items of size $k$ from a larger set of $n$ items based on pooled tests. We introduce a selectable threshold model, in which each test has an associated threshold that can be chosen, such that the test outcome is 1 if and only if the number of defectives in the test is no smaller than that threshold. In settings with a large or unbounded maximum threshold, we establish conditions under which high-probability recovery can be attained with a rate (i.e., the asymptotic ratio of $\log_2{n \choose k}$ to the number of tests) approaching its maximum possible value of 1. Moreover, in the case of a fixed maximum threshold, we establish an achievable number of tests using simple and computationally efficient decoding methods, and a converse that holds under suitable regularity conditions on the test design, with the two coinciding in the dense limit (i.e., $θ$ approaching one in the scaling $k = Θ(n^θ)$).

</details>


### [251] [Quasi-Belief Propagation and Neural-Network Check Node Processing for BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14589)
*Guangwen Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于BCH码的准BP译码方案，该方案保留了置信传播的可并行结构，同时利用码自同构和优化的冗余校验矩阵。进一步引入基于轻量级CNN的神经网络变体替换tanh/tanh⁻¹函数，通过三重约束损失函数训练。仿真表明准BP译码在FER性能上与类似码长的LDPC码BP译码差距在0.25dB以内，神经网络变体性能损失可忽略，级联OSD变体可进一步逼近ML界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCH码的传统BP译码存在计算复杂度高（tanh/tanh⁻¹函数）以及译码性能与LDPC码存在差距的问题，因此需要一种兼顾并行性、低复杂度和高性能的译码方案，以满足下一代通信系统高吞吐量、低延迟的需求。

Method: 提出准BP译码方案，保留BP可并行结构，利用码自同构和优化的冗余校验矩阵；进一步提出神经网络变体，用轻量级CNN替换tanh/tanh⁻¹函数，并采用三重约束损失函数（非负性和顺序一致性）进行训练；最后级联OSD变体以逼近ML界。

Result: 对三种BCH码的仿真表明：准BP译码的FER性能与类似码长的LDPC码BP译码差距在0.25dB以内；神经网络变体性能损失可忽略，且能在硬件加速器上稳定部署；级联OSD变体进一步缩小了与ML界的差距。

Conclusion: 所提出的方案为下一代通信系统中BCH码的高吞吐量、低延迟译码提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a quasi-BP decoding scheme for BCH codes that preserves the parallelizable structure of belief propagation while exploiting code automorphisms and optimized redundant parity-check matrices. To eliminate the computationally expensive $\tanh$ and $\tanh^{-1}$ functions in check node updates, we further introduce a neural-network-based variant that replaces them with a lightweight convolutional neural network trained under a triple-constraint loss function enforcing non-negativity and order consistency. Simulation results for three BCH codes demonstrate that quasi-BP decoding achieves competitive frame error rate performance, with a gap within 0.25 decibels compared with belief propagation decoding of an LDPC code of similar blocklength. The neural-network-based variant incurs negligible performance loss while enabling stable deployment with arithmetic operations on hardware accelerators. Concatenation with an ordered statistics decoding variant further bridges the gap to the maximum-likelihood bound. Hence, the proposed schemes offer a viable path toward high-throughput, low-latency decoding of BCH codes in next-generation communication systems.

</details>


### [252] [Identification Codes and Post-Shannon Communication: Theory, Architectures, and Emerging Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14666)
*Wafa Labidi,Kumar Nilesh,Johannes Rosenberger,Juan Cabrera,Holger Boche,Christian Deppe,Frank H. P. Fitzek,Marc Geitz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述了由Ahlswede和Dueck提出的识别（ID）编码理论，该理论通过假设检验替代消息重建，实现了码本数量随块长双指数增长的特性。文章重点讨论了ID编码在监控、报警、控制系统等无需完整消息重建场景中的应用，并介绍了联合识别与感知（JIDAS）、语义通信、移动网络控制等新兴架构与实际系统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典通信范式要求完整重建发送消息，但在监控、报警等大规模分布式场景中，接收端仅需判断特定消息是否被发送，无需完整解码。ID编码通过假设检验替代消息重建，利用双指数增长的码本容量，为后香农时代的大规模通信系统提供了全新的架构基础。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理ID编码的理论基础（包括双指数容量增长的数学原理），并归纳其在监控系统、专用数据存储、联合识别与感知（JIDAS）、语义通信、移动网络控制系统和网络化一致性测试系统等场景中的应用架构，同时介绍系统概念、工业视角和实现示例。

Result: ID编码的核心优势在于码本数量随块长呈双指数增长（即码本大小为2^(2^n)量级），远优于经典通信的指数增长。该特性使得ID编码特别适合大规模分布式系统中仅需判断特定消息是否发送的场景，如监控、报警、控制等。

Conclusion: ID编码虽不能替代经典通信，但在无需完整消息重建的场景中具有显著优势。本文涵盖的理论基础、应用架构和实现案例表明，ID编码原理能够在实际通信系统中落地，为后香农通信时代提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Identification (ID) coding, introduced by Ahlswede and Dueck, extends Shannon's classical communication paradigm by replacing message reconstruction with hypothesis testing. Instead of decoding the transmitted message, the receiver only decides whether a particular message was sent. A fundamental result of ID theory is the double-exponential growth in the number of identifiable messages with respect to (w.r.t.) the blocklength. This scaling behavior enables fundamentally new communication architectures for large-scale distributed systems and forms a key building block of post-Shannon communication.
  While ID cannot replace classical communication in general, it is particularly well-suited for scenarios in which full message reconstruction is unnecessary, such as monitoring, alarming, and control systems.
  In this survey, we review the theoretical foundations of ID coding and discuss emerging communication architectures and application domains based on this paradigm. Particular emphasis is placed on practical use cases, including monitoring systems, special-purpose data storage, joint identification and sensing (JIDAS), semantic communications, mobile-network control systems and networked consensus testing systems. We further highlight recent system concepts, industrial perspectives, and implementation examples that illustrate how ID-based principles can be realized in practical communication systems.

</details>


### [253] [Statistics of the Compression Ratio of a Variable-to-Variable Code: Exact Moments and Asymptotic Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14676)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对变长到变长(V2V)码，推导了有限样本压缩比$R_n$的各阶矩精确公式，并基于Edgeworth近似、拉普拉斯方法等给出了比中心极限定理更精确的分布近似，以及偏差常数和方差常数的闭式表达式，并扩展到马尔可夫源，建立了编码理论下界和偏差系数的结构分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2V码的实际压缩性能由有限样本的压缩比$R_n$衡量，而不仅仅是渐近速率$\rho$。然而现有理论主要关注$\rho$，对$R_n$的分布性质（如均值、方差、偏差）缺乏精确刻画，因此需要建立$R_n$的有限样本统计分析框架。

Method: 1) 利用单短语的$(L,\ell)$矩生成函数，推导$R_n$各阶矩的闭式一维积分公式；2) 基于矩的Edgeworth展开逼近$R_n$的累积分布函数；3) 利用拉普拉斯积分方法得到偏差常数$C$和方差常数的显式闭式公式；4) 将V2V码建模为有限状态编码器，应用广义Kraft不等式给出压缩率下界；5) 对偏差系数进行结构分解，分离出V2F、F2V和V2V的贡献。

Result: 获得了$R_n$各阶矩的精确积分公式；Edgeworth近似比CLT近似显著更准确；得到了偏差常数和方差常数的闭式表达式；针对马尔可夫源给出状态索引矩阵形式的冗余公式；通过偏差系数的结构分解，揭示了Khodak码的改进性能体现在其更小的偏差常数上。

Conclusion: 该研究为V2V码的有限样本性能提供了完整的理论分析工具，填补了从渐近速率到有限样本分布之间的理论空白，并对理解不同编码结构（V2F/F2V/V2V）在偏差上的贡献提供了新视角。

Abstract: A variable-to-variable (V2V) length code parses a source sequence into phrases of variable length and maps each phrase to a binary codeword of, generally, a different random length. After encoding $n$ phrases, the realized compression ratio $R_n=Λ_n/Σ_n$ -- total codeword length over total source-symbol count -- is the finite-sample counterpart of the code's asymptotic rate $ρ$, to which it converges only as $n\to\infty$. This paper first derives exact formulas for all integer moments of $R_n$ for a given discrete memoryless source (DMS). Specifically, we obtain a closed-form formula for every moment $\E\{R_n^k\}$ as a one-dimensional integral involving only single-phrase moment generating functions of the pair $(L,\ell)$ -- the phrase length, in source symbols, and codeword length, in bits. From these moments we derive an Edgeworth approximation to the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of $R_n$ that is substantially more accurate than the central limit theorem (CLT) approximation. Using the Laplace method of integration, we also derive explicit closed-form formulas for the bias constant $C=\lim_{n\to\infty}n(\E\{R_n\}-ρ)$ and for the variance constant $\lim_{n\to\infty}n\cdot\Var\{R_n\}$. The analysis extends to Markov sources via state-indexed matrices with a redundancy formula obtained in closed form. On the coding-theoretic side, we cast V2V length codes as finite-state encoders and apply a generalized Kraft inequality for a compression-rate lower bound, and give a structural decomposition of the bias coefficient that separates cleanly across variable-to-fixed (V2F) length codes, fixed-to-variable (F2V) length codes, and V2V length codes. Applied to the Khodak code of Bugeaud, Drmota, and Szpankowski, this decomposition shows that its improved performance is reflected in its smaller bias constant.

</details>


### [254] [Random Spherical Codes at High SNR: Error Transitions, Fixed-Error Data Rates, and Converse Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14768)
*Nikola Zlatanov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了高信噪比、固定块长下随机球形码本在AWGN信道中的纠错性能，发现了错误概率的剧烈相变现象，并推导了可达速率与信道容量界的差距随块长和错误概率的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高信噪比区域，当块长固定而码本大小随信噪比增长时，随机球形码本的误差性能尚不明确，特别是码本增长尺度与错误概率之间的相变关系有待刻画。

Method: 采用随机球形码本模型，分析固定块长下码本大小随信噪比增长时的平均错误概率，通过识别码本增长临界尺度，推导错误概率的渐近行为，并通过反演相变规律得到可达速率的高信噪比展开。

Result: 发现了错误概率在临界码本增长尺度处的相变现象：低于临界尺度时错误概率趋于0，等于临界尺度时趋于非平凡极限，高于临界尺度时趋于1。可达速率与信道容量界的比值随信噪比趋于1，但差值趋于一个依赖于块长和错误概率的正常数极限。

Conclusion: 随机球形码本在高信噪比下具有与信道容量界相同的领先阶增长率，可靠性通过常数项体现；当块长足够大时，可达速率与容量界的差距可趋近于0，但若错误概率随块长下降，该差距可能不趋于0。

Abstract: This paper characterizes random spherical codebooks over the real additive white Gaussian noise channel in the high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime when the blocklength is fixed and the codebook size grows with SNR. In this regime, the random spherical ensemble exhibits a sharp error-probability transition governed by the intrinsic dimension of the sphere and the codebook-growth scale. Below the critical codebook-growth scale, the ensemble-average error probability vanishes; at the critical scale, it converges to a nontrivial limit; and above that scale, it approaches one. By inverting this transition law, we obtain the high-SNR expansion of the ensemble-achievable data rate for a prescribed error probability. This rate has the same leading high-SNR growth as the corresponding converse rate bound, while reliability enters through the constant-order terms. Consequently, the ratio of the ensemble-achievable rate to the converse rate bound tends to one as the SNR increases. Their additive difference, however, generally approaches a positive blocklength- and reliability-dependent limit. We characterize this limiting rate-bound gap jointly as a function of blocklength and error probability. For every fixed error probability, the gap vanishes as the blocklength increases. We further identify the reliability scalings under which a decreasing error probability prevents the additive gap from vanishing in the large-blocklength limit.

</details>


### [255] [Lossy compression of weighted graph adjacency matrices by transform coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14834)
*Kenta Yanagiya,Junya Hara,Hiroshi Higashi,Yuichi Tanaka,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对加权图的压缩框架，其中图拓扑无损传输，边权进行有损压缩。通过将图转换为线图，将边权视为线图上的图信号，利用图滤波器组进行变换后量化编码，同时提出了边平滑度作为压缩难度的度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权图压缩中边权有损压缩的挑战在于边之间潜在关系的模糊性，现有方法未能有效利用边之间的关系进行压缩。

Method: 1) 将原始图转换为线图，线图节点对应原始图的边，线图边编码原始图边之间的关系；2) 将边权向量视为线图上的图信号；3) 在线图上使用图滤波器组对边权向量进行变换；4) 对变换系数进行量化和熵编码；5) 形式化定义线图上的边平滑度，作为压缩难度的度量。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验验证了所提方法相比现有矩阵预处理方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 线图变换能将边权有损压缩问题转化为图信号处理问题，边平滑度可无需转换线图直接计算，为加权图压缩性能提供预判依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a compression framework for weighted graphs in which the graph topology is transmitted losslessly and edge weights are compressed lossily. A challenge in the lossy compression of edge weights is that the underlying relationships between edges are ambiguous. To address this issue, we first transform the unweighted graph into the corresponding line graph, whose nodes represent the edges of the original graph and whose edges encode the relationships between them. The line graph transform allows us to regard edge weights as a graph signal defined on the line graph. Instead of transmitting the edge-weight vector, we first transform it with a graph filter bank on the line graph. Then, quantization and entropy coding are performed on the transformed coefficients of the edge weight vector. In addition to the lossy compression method, we formalize edge smoothness on the line graph and show that it serves as a measure of the difficulty of compression. The proposed smoothness measure can be easily calculated without converting to a line graph. This provides insight into the expected compression performance of a given weighted graph. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data validate the effectiveness of the proposed method by comparing it with existing matrix preprocessing methods.

</details>


### [256] [Covering Sequences and Covering-Sequences Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14840)
*Tuvi Etzion*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了覆盖序列和覆盖序列码，利用汉明码构造了短长度、小半径的覆盖序列，以及序列数少、总码字数少的覆盖序列码，并给出了渐近最优的长序列构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖序列在编码理论中有重要应用，而$(n,R)$-覆盖序列及其码是构建更优覆盖序列的最佳构件。需要系统的方法来构造具有小半径、短长度以及较少序列数的覆盖序列和码。

Method: 利用汉明码进行构造：对于小半径，构造短长度的覆盖序列；对于足够大的素数幂字母表，构造渐近最优的长序列；并将相同构造推广到较大半径情形。

Result: 得到了短长度、小半径的覆盖序列，以及序列数少、总码字数少的$(n,m,R)$-覆盖序列码；对于足够大的素数幂字母表，构造了渐近最优的长序列；相同构造在较大半径下也得到有趣的码。

Conclusion: 汉明码是构造覆盖序列和覆盖序列码的有效工具，能同时实现短长度、小半径、少序列数的目标，且对于大字母表可达到渐近最优性。

Abstract: An $(n,R)$-covering sequence is a cyclic sequence whose consecutive $n$-tuples form a code of length $n$ and covering radius $R$. An $(n,m,R)$-covering-sequences code is a set of cyclic sequences of length $m$, whose consecutive $n$-tuples form a code of length $n$ and covering radius $R$. These codes are the best building blocks for $(n,R)$-covering sequences. We show, for small radii, how the Hamming code can be used to construct such sequences of short length and such codes with a relatively small number of sequences and a total number of codewords. Sequences with small radius whose length approaches asymptotically to optimality are constructed, especially for an alphabet of prime power size large enough. With the same construction, interesting codes are also constructed for larger radii.

</details>


### [257] [Quantum XYZ Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14988)
*Alessio Baldelli,Davide Orsucci,Francisco Lázaro,Massimo Battaglioni*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出量子XYZ稳定子码，其校验矩阵由X、Y、Z三型稳定子生成元的三个两两正交二进制校验矩阵构成，分析了其非CSS特性的判定条件，推导了量子最小距离的上下界，并通过仿真展示了该类码在有限长度下优于代表性CSS qLDPC码的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CSS框架虽然代数上便利，但引入非CSS约束有助于抑制低权重逻辑算符并提升有限长度下的译码性能，因此需要构建和系统研究非CSS结构的量子码。

Method: 利用三个两两正交二进制校验矩阵分别对应X、Y、Z型稳定子生成元构建量子XYZ稳定子码；给出判断Y型校验是否冗余以及码是否真正非CSS的代数与秩条件；推导量子最小距离的上下界（包括混合Pauli逻辑算符情形）；采用相交子集和拟对偶码族构造稀疏有限长度qLDPC码。

Result: XYZ框架包含了已知的非CSS拓扑码（XYZ²六边形码）；构造的有限长度XYZ qLDPC实例在退极化信道容量噪声下使用四元置信传播译码，性能优于代表性的CSS qLDPC实例。

Conclusion: XYZ稳定子码提供了一种系统化的非CSS量子码构造框架，在有限长度下具有译码性能优势，且可通过秩条件明确区分真正的非CSS码与可退化为CSS的码。

Abstract: Stabilizer codes are often constructed within the Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) framework, where two mutually orthogonal binary classical codes define $X$ and $Z$-type stabilizer generators. While this structure is algebraically convenient, additional non-CSS constraints may help suppress low-weight logical operators and improve decoding performance in the finite-length regime. We thus introduce quantum XYZ stabilizer codes, whose parity-check matrix (PCM) is built from three pairwise orthogonal binary PCMs associated with $X$-, $Y$-, and $Z$-type stabilizer generators. A nontrivial point is that an XYZ code instance is not automatically genuinely non-CSS: the same stabilizer group may admit a CSS generating set. We characterize this collapse, obtaining algebraic and rank conditions for deciding when the $Y$-type checks are redundant and when they define genuinely non-CSS stabilizer constraints. We also derive upper and lower bounds on the quantum minimum distance, including bounds for mixed Pauli logical operators. The novel framework includes a known non-CSS topological code, namely the XYZ$^2$ hexagonal code, and yields also sparse finite-length quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) constructions from intersecting-subset and quasi-dyadic code families. Simulations under depolarizing code-capacity noise and quaternary belief propagation decoding show that the proposed XYZ qLDPC instances can outperform representative CSS qLDPC instances with similar finite-length parameters.

</details>
